A KOReader plugin records every reading session and syncs positions both ways, powering streaks, pace, heatmaps and per-book stats. ([Source Code (⭐108)](https://github.com/bndct-devops/tome), [Clients (⭐108)](https://github.com/bndct-devops/tome/blob/main/docs/koreader-plugin.md)) `AGPL-3.0` `Docker`

*   [s3-orchestrator](https://s3-orchestrator.munchbox.cc) - Storage orchestration layer presenting many cloud providers as a single S3 endpoint, keeping N copies across them with read failover, plus optional per-backend byte, request, ingress and egress limits, zstd compression and envelope encryption. ([Source Code (⭐45)](https://github.com/afreidah/s3-orchestrator)) `MIT` `Go/Docker/deb/K8S`

##### Software / File Transfer - Peer-to-peer Filesharing

*   [DashBeam](https://dashbeam.net) - File transfer tool with peer-to-peer networking, letting you transfer any file with any size. ([Demo](https://app.dashbeam.net/), [Source Code (⭐10k)](https://github.com/tonyantony300/dashbeam)) `AGPL-3.0` `deb/Docker`

#### [5. Awesome Web Security](/content/qazbnm456/awesome-web-security/README.md)

##### Fingerprint

*   [Passive OS Fingerprinting: TCP Fingerprint, p0f and JA4T](https://packet.guru/blog/passive-os-fingerprinting) - How the first TCP SYN gives away the operating system family, why a proxy shows up as a mismatch with the OS the browser reports, and how p0f and JA4T record the same packet. Written by [packet.guru](https://packet.guru/).

##### JWT

*   [How to revoke a JWT, from a system that does it](https://easinvy.com/notes/revoke-a-jwt) - A per-user revocation cut-off from a production multi-tenant app: why it compares against `auth_time` rather than `iat`, the whole-seconds rounding bug it shipped with, and the routes that trusted the token. Written by [Ridam Agrawal](https://ridamagrawal.com).

##### SSRF

*   [Instance Metadata Abuse](https://ransomleak.com/exercises/instance-metadata-abuse/) - Shows how a server-side request forgery bug becomes cloud credential theft through the instance metadata service, and why requiring IMDSv2 session tokens with a response hop limit in the instance configuration closes the whole class of forging bugs rather than one code path, by [RansomLeak](https://ransomleak.com/).

##### Scanning / Sub Domain Enumeration

*   [ONUS (⭐12)](https://github.com/maverickaayush/ONUS) - Web vulnerability assessment platform that orchestrates multiple scanners, applies deterministic CVSS v3.1 scoring, and provides AI-assisted vulnerability analysis and remediation guidance.
*   [Crossfyre Toolchain (⭐256)](https://github.com/clickswave/crossfyre-toolchain) - Recon and vulnerability scanning engines in Rust for subdomains, ports, content discovery, fingerprinting, injection testing and nuclei-style templates, with one CLI and newline-delimited JSON output by [@clickswave](https://github.com/clickswave).

##### Decompiler / Server-Side Request Forgery

*   [Wakaru (⭐1.1k)](https://github.com/pionxzh/wakaru) - JavaScript decompiler that splits production bundles from webpack, esbuild, Browserify, and Metro into modules and turns minified and transpiled code back into readable JavaScript, without executing it, by [@pionxzh](https://github.com/pionxzh).

#### [8. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

*   [Joxo](https://joxo.ai?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Shared project for a team's coding agents. Start Gemini CLI with `joxo gemini` and it works from the same task board, decisions and handoffs as teammates' Claude Code, Codex or Cursor agents, and can ask a teammate's agent for help. Desktop app, iPhone app and a remote MCP server.
*   [EndoStack (⭐3)](https://github.com/requie/endostack) - Security-first workflows for coding agents: 62 skills that threat-model changes, security-review diffs and verify results by running them. Works in Gemini CLI, Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and 11 other agents. MIT.
*   [Preloop (⭐85)](https://github.com/preloop/preloop) - Open-source AI agent control plane and software factory: every tool and model call allowed, denied or held for a human, budgeted and recorded, and agents run from tickets, webhooks and CI in hosted containers or on your own runners, landing work as pull requests. Works with Gemini CLI, Claude Code, Codex CLI, Cursor, OpenCode and Copilot CLI. Apache-2.0.
*   [AgentVera](https://agentvera.dev/en/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Desktop app for macOS, Windows and Linux that runs Gemini CLI side by side with Claude Code, Codex, Copilot, Cursor and 13 more coding agents per project, each in a real terminal using your own Gemini CLI install and login. A team kanban board hands cards to agents, and an MCP manager is built in. Closed source; free plan, Pro $9.99/month.

##### Commands & Extensions

*   [Ponder (⭐3)](https://github.com/Quinnod345/ponder) - Gemini CLI extension and Agent Skill for multi-pass deliberation and code review: grades every file or document across 6 dimensions, runs four adversarial perspectives (skeptic, builder, dreamer, empath), and blocks early stopping until coverage reaches 1.00 and churn converges below 0.02 across two consecutive passes. Zero runtime dependencies, MIT. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/Quinnod345/ponder` or `npx skills add Quinnod345/ponder -y -a gemini-cli`.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

*   [Skim (⭐16)](https://github.com/skim-md/skim) - Browser extension that renders the Markdown plans and docs Gemini CLI writes, re-rendering live as the file changes. KaTeX, Mermaid, folder view.

##### Education & Study Tools

*   [mock-interview (⭐2)](https://github.com/georgesmomo/mock-interview) - Agent Skill that runs in Gemini CLI and other agents (`npx skills add georgesmomo/mock-interview`). Runs a realistic mock job interview for any role, with research on the job and real follow-up questions, then gives an honest score out of 10 and a written report.

##### MCP Servers

*   [mach-gtm-mcp (⭐1)](https://github.com/verma6uc/mach-gtm-mcp) - Open-source B2B outbound sales MCP server: build ICPs, research prospects with verified buying signals, score leads (0-100), draft cold emails and LinkedIn messages (1-3-1 framework), and generate multi-touch sequences. Human approval required before any message is sent. Works with Gemini CLI and any MCP client. Install: `npx mach-gtm-mcp`.
*   [Keenable (⭐2)](https://github.com/keenableai/keenable-antigravity) - Web search and page fetch as clean Markdown from Keenable, a search engine with its own crawler and index built for AI agents. Gemini CLI extension that adds Keenable's hosted MCP server (`search_web_pages`, `fetch_page_content`) and a web-lookup skill; keyless, no signup or API key (rate limited per IP). MIT. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/keenableai/keenable-antigravity`.
*   [repofit (⭐4)](https://github.com/pth2002/repofit) - MCP server and CLI that reads what local coding agents already keep (Gemini CLI chats and `GEMINI.md`, plus Codex, Claude Code, Cursor and others) and recommends GitHub projects for the tasks you repeat, each pick citing the session it came from. Also lists installed MCP servers and skills that no session ever called. Runs locally and uses the agent's own model, so no API key. MIT. Add with `gemini mcp add repofit uvx repofit mcp`.
*   [AlphaOSINT LinkedIn Company](https://alphaosint.com/sources/linkedin-company-api/) - Remote MCP server for Gemini CLI that returns a company's LinkedIn page details (size, industry, locations, specialties) through `https://mcp.apify.com/?tools=johnvc/linkedin-company-api`.

*   [OHDSI ATLAS (⭐335)](https://github.com/OHDSI/Atlas) - Web tool for observational research on OMOP CDM data.

##### Contents / Integration

*   [LinuxForHealth HL7v2-FHIR Converter (⭐110)](https://github.com/LinuxForHealth/hl7v2-fhir-converter) - Converts HL7 v2 messages to FHIR resources.
*   [mirthSync (⭐104)](https://github.com/SagaHealthcareIT/mirthsync) - CLI for putting Mirth Connect and Open Integration Engine configuration under version control.
*   [Open Integration Engine (⭐240)](https://github.com/OpenIntegrationEngine/engine) - Community open source fork of Mirth Connect.

##### Contents / Bioinformatics

*   [cBioPortal (⭐1.1k)](https://github.com/cBioPortal/cbioportal) - Platform for exploring and visualizing cancer genomics data.
*   [GATK (⭐2k)](https://github.com/broadinstitute/gatk) - Broad Institute toolkit for variant discovery in high-throughput sequencing data.
*   [Hail (⭐1.1k)](https://github.com/hail-is/hail) - Scalable genomic data analysis.
*   [nf-core/sarek (⭐616)](https://github.com/nf-core/sarek) - Pipeline to detect germline and somatic variants from sequencing data.
*   [scikit-bio (⭐1.3k)](https://github.com/scikit-bio/scikit-bio) - Python library for bioinformatics, providing tools for sequence analysis, microbiome studies, and statistical analysis of biological data.

##### Contents / Data

*   [DHIS2 (⭐360)](https://github.com/dhis2/dhis2-core) - Health information management platform used by national health systems.
*   [medspaCy (⭐684)](https://github.com/medspacy/medspacy) - Library for clinical NLP with spaCy.
*   [OpenMRS FHIR Analytics (⭐228)](https://github.com/google/fhir-data-pipes) - Pipeline tools for transforming OpenMRS data to FHIR-based analytics using Apache Beam.
*   [Pathling (⭐140)](https://github.com/aehrc/pathling) - FHIR and clinical terminology analytics on Apache Spark.
*   [Snowstorm (⭐399)](https://github.com/IHTSDO/snowstorm) - SNOMED International's SNOMED CT terminology server.
*   [Tuva (⭐333)](https://github.com/tuva-health/tuva-core) - Open source data model, data marts and data quality tests for claims and clinical data.

##### Contents / Machine learning

*   [nnU-Net (⭐9k)](https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/nnUNet) - Self-configuring deep learning framework for biomedical image segmentation.
*   [Odyssey (⭐63)](https://github.com/VectorInstitute/odyssey) - Interpretable foundation model of the patient clinical timeline for event forecasting and time-to-event prediction.
*   [OpenMed (⭐5.8k)](https://github.com/maziyarpanahi/openmed) - Local clinical named-entity recognition and PHI de-identification models.
*   [PathML (⭐467)](https://github.com/Dana-Farber-AIOS/PathML) - Python library for digital pathology image analysis, providing tools for preprocessing, model training, and analysis of histological slides.
*   [seismometer (⭐272)](https://github.com/epic-open-source/seismometer) - Epic's framework for evaluating AI models in healthcare.
*   [TorchIO (⭐2.5k)](https://github.com/TorchIO-project/torchio) - Medical image preprocessing and augmentation for deep learning.
*   [TotalSegmentator (⭐3.1k)](https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator) - Segmentation of more than 100 anatomical structures in CT and MR images.

##### Contents / Compliance

*   [HealthStack (⭐94)](https://github.com/the-momentum/healthstack) - Terraform modules for secure and compliant healthcare infrastructure on AWS.

#### [14. Awesome Privacy](/content/pluja/awesome-privacy/README.md)

##### Medical health / Apps and services

*   **[MedTimer (⭐649)](https://github.com/Futsch1/medTimer)** - Medication reminder that keeps all data on the device and has no internet permission. <sub>Android · MIT · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.futsch1.medtimer/)</sub>

##### Video and Audio Conferencing / Desktop

*   **WhatsApp**: shares metadata such as who you call and when with Meta. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/198)</sub>
*   **Zoom**: claimed end-to-end encryption that its meetings didn't have. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2198) · [Source](https://theintercept.com/2020/03/31/zoom-meeting-encryption/)</sub>
*   **[BigBlueButton](https://bigbluebutton.org/)** - Self-hosted web conferencing built for online classes, with whiteboard, breakout rooms and recordings. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>self-host · LGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Briefing](https://brie.fi/ng)** - Group video chat in the browser over direct WebRTC connections, with no account. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Chitchatter](https://chitchatter.im/)** - Serverless, ephemeral peer-to-peer chat with audio, video, screen and file sharing. <sub>GPL-2.0</sub>
*   **[Jami](https://jami.net/)** - Peer-to-peer audio and video calls and chat with no central servers or account needed. <sub>GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/cx.ring/)</sub>
*   **[Jitsi Meet](https://jitsi.org/jitsi-meet/)** - Video conferencing you can self-host; the official instance requires you to log in. <sub>self-host · Apache-2.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.jitsi.meet/) (Play Store version has trackers)</sub>
*   **[MiroTalk P2P](https://p2p.mirotalk.com/)** - Browser-based peer-to-peer video calls up to 4K, with nothing to install. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Mumble](https://www.mumble.info/)** - Low-latency voice chat with encrypted connections, on servers you can run yourself. <sub>self-host</sub>

##### Instant Messaging

*   **Facebook Messenger**: Meta's messenger, with 5 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/182) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.facebook.orca/latest/)</sub>

##### Instagram / Alternative Medium frontends

*   **Instagram**: Meta builds an ad profile from what you view, like and follow. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/219)</sub>
*   **[Pixelfed](https://pixelfed.org/)** - Federated photo sharing with posts, stories and a chronological feed, without ads. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Location tracking / Apps and services

*   **Google Find Hub (formerly Find My Device)**: reports your devices' locations to Google. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/217)</sub>

##### Centralized

*   **[Signal](https://signal.org/)** - Messenger with end-to-end encryption for everything by default, run by a nonprofit that keeps almost no user data. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Threema](https://threema.com/en)** - Paid messenger from Switzerland that works without a phone number or email. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Maps and Navigation / Apps and services

*   **Bing Maps**: under Microsoft's very poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/244)</sub>
*   **Yandex Maps**: 11 trackers in its Android app, from a company bound by Russian surveillance law. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/ru.yandex.yandexmaps/latest/)</sub>
*   **Sygic**: 8 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.sygic.aura/latest/)</sub>
*   **[OpenStreetMap (OSM)](https://www.openstreetmap.org/)** - Collaborative world map built by volunteers, and the open data behind most apps in this section. <sub>GPL-2.0</sub>
    *   **[OsmAnd](https://osmand.net/)** - Offline maps and navigation built on OpenStreetMap, with a large set of features. `⚠️ telemetry opt-out` <sub>Android · iOS · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/net.osmand.plus/)</sub>
*   **[Pure Maps](https://rinigus.github.io/pure-maps/)** - Maps and navigation for Linux phones, Sailfish OS and desktop, fully offline with OSM Scout Server. <sub>Linux · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Search Engines / Useful Tools

*   **Yahoo!**: logs your searches under a very poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/309)</sub>
*   **Yandex**: Russian law gives the security services access to user data. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/860)</sub>
*   **[Brave Search](https://search.brave.com)** - Search engine with [its own independent index](https://brave.com/search-independence/), from the makers of Brave.
*   **[DuckDuckGo](https://duckduckgo.com)** - Search engine that doesn't build a profile on you, with results drawn largely from Bing.
*   **[Marginalia](https://marginalia-search.com/)** - Independent search engine with its own crawler and index that favors text-heavy, non-commercial pages. <sub>self-host</sub>
*   **[Mojeek](https://www.mojeek.com/)** - UK search engine with its own crawler and index and a no-tracking policy.
*   **[Qwant](https://www.qwant.com/)** - French search engine, hosted in the EU, that doesn't track your searches.
*   **[SearXNG](https://docs.searxng.org/)** - Metasearch engine that queries other search services for you; use a [public instance](https://searx.space/) or host your own. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[YaCy](https://yacy.net/)** - Peer-to-peer search engine where every user runs a node and shares the index.

##### Third-Party owned

*   **[Posteo](https://posteo.de/en)** - Ad-free email from Germany, funded only by its users, with optional encrypted storage and sign-up and payment that need no personal details.
*   **[Proton Mail](https://proton.me/mail)** - Email from Switzerland, end-to-end encrypted between Proton users and with PGP, and stored encrypted otherwise, where a court order can still force it to log your IP ([2021 case](https://proton.me/blog/climate-activist-arrest)). <sub>GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Self-Hosted

*   **[Docker Mailserver](https://docker-mailserver.github.io/docker-mailserver/latest/)** - Complete mail server (SMTP, IMAP, antispam, antivirus) in a single Docker container, configured with files. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[Mail-in-a-box](https://mailinabox.email/)** - Turns a fresh Ubuntu server into a complete mail server with one setup script. <sub>self-host · CC0-1.0</sub>
*   **[Mailcow: dockerized](https://mailcow.email)** - Docker-based mail server suite with a web admin panel, webmail and groupware. <sub>self-host · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Mox](https://www.xmox.nl)** - Self-contained mail server in a single binary, built to need little maintenance. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>

##### Clients / Android / iOS

*   **[FairEmail](https://email.faircode.eu/)** - Full-featured email app that blocks tracking images and keeps everything on your device. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/eu.faircode.email/)</sub>
*   **[Thunderbird for Android](https://www.thunderbird.net/en-US/mobile/)** - Mozilla's email app for Android, built on K-9 Mail, which still ships from the same code. <sub>Android · Apache-2.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/net.thunderbird.android/)</sub>

##### Clients / Desktop

*   **[NeoMutt](https://neomutt.org/)** - Terminal email client with built-in PGP/GPG support, a maintained fork of Mutt. <sub>Linux · macOS · CLI · GPL-2.0</sub>

##### Email Alias Services (Anonymous Forwarding) / Desktop

*   **[addy.io](https://addy.io)** - Email alias and forwarding service you can host yourself, formerly AnonAddy. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[SimpleLogin](https://simplelogin.io)** - Email alias service you can host yourself, owned by Proton. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Teamworking Tools / Desktop

*   **Discord**: messages aren't end-to-end encrypted, under a very poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/536)</sub>
*   **Slack**: messages aren't end-to-end encrypted, and workspace admins can export them. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/206)</sub>
*   **[Rocket.Chat](https://www.rocket.chat/)** - Self-hosted team chat with end-to-end encrypted rooms and federation. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>self-host</sub>
*   **[Twake Chat](https://twake.app/)** - Workplace chat built on Matrix, part of Linagora's Twake Workplace suite. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Wire](https://wire.com/)** - End-to-end encrypted messaging, calls and file sharing for teams, with public and audited code for both the apps and the server. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>AGPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.wire/) (Play Store version has trackers)</sub>

##### Alternative clients/modifications of Discord / Desktop

*   **[Aliucord (⭐4.9k)](https://github.com/Aliucord/Aliucord)** - Mod for the Android Discord app that [disables Discord's tracking (⭐4.9k)](https://github.com/Aliucord/Aliucord/blob/main/Aliucord/src/main/java/com/aliucord/coreplugins/NoTrack.java). <sub>Android · OSL-3.0</sub>
*   **[BetterDiscord](https://betterdiscord.app/)** - Discord client mod; add the [DoNotTrack](https://betterdiscord.app/plugins/DoNotTrack) plugin to block trackers. <sub>Apache-2.0</sub>
*   **[Legcord](https://legcord.app)** - Lightweight Discord client that blocks Discord's known trackers by default, formerly ArmCord. <sub>OSL-3.0</sub>
*   **[OpenAsar](https://openasar.dev/)** - Drop-in replacement for part of the Discord desktop app, with a [No Tracking (⭐3k)](https://github.com/GooseMod/OpenAsar#readme) option that turns off crash and error reporting. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Replugged](https://replugged.dev/)** - Discord client mod that continues the discontinued Powercord. <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[Vencord](https://vencord.dev)** - Discord client mod with plugins, including one that blocks Discord's tracking. <sub>GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Vesktop](https://vesktop.dev/)** - Desktop Discord client with Vencord built in, which blocks Discord's telemetry. <sub>GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[WebCord (⭐2.5k)](https://github.com/SpacingBat3/WebCord)** - Discord client that loads the web app in Electron instead of using the Discord API, with extra privacy settings. <sub>MIT</sub>

##### Blogging platforms (Medium) / Desktop

*   **Medium**: the site carries trackers, under a poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/1003)</sub>
*   **[Plume](https://joinplu.me/)** - Federated blogging engine where readers can follow and comment from any ActivityPub account. `💀 inactive` <sub>ActivityPub</sub>
*   **[WriteFreely](https://writefreely.org/)** - Minimal, distraction-free blogging platform that can federate posts over ActivityPub. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### YouTube / Alternative Medium frontends

*   **YouTube**: Google builds a detailed profile from everything you watch. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/274)</sub>
*   **[PeerTube](https://joinpeertube.org/en/)** - Federated video hosting where anyone can run an instance and follow channels across others. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### YouTube / YouTube alternative frontends (web-based)

*   **[Invidious](https://invidious.io/)** - YouTube frontend with no ads or Google scripts and subscriptions without an account; see the [public instances](https://docs.invidious.io/instances/). <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[youtube-local (⭐779)](https://github.com/user234683/youtube-local)** - YouTube client that runs on your computer and opens in the browser, with local subscriptions and optional routing through Tor. <sub>Desktop · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### YouTube / YouTube alternative clients (apps)

*   **[FreeTube](https://freetubeapp.io/)** - Desktop YouTube app that keeps subscriptions, playlists and history on your computer, without Google's cookies or scripts. <sub>Desktop · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[LibreTube](https://libretube.dev/)** - Android YouTube app that fetches videos through Piped instead of Google's servers. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.github.libretube/)</sub>
*   **[NewPipe](https://newpipe.net/)** - Lightweight Android YouTube app with background play and downloads, no Google services or account needed. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.schabi.newpipe/)</sub>
*   **[SkyTube (⭐2.8k)](https://github.com/SkyTubeTeam/SkyTube)** - Android YouTube app with local subscriptions, downloads and a channel and video blocker. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/free.rm.skytube.oss/)</sub>
*   **[Yattee (⭐3.8k)](https://github.com/yattee/yattee)** - Native YouTube player for iPhone, iPad, Apple TV and Mac that works through Invidious or Piped. <sub>iOS · macOS · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Twitter / YouTube alternative clients (apps)

*   **Twitter (X)**: profiles you from who you follow and what you like and repost. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/195)</sub>

##### Twitter / Decentralized

*   **[Mastodon](https://joinmastodon.org/)** - Federated microblogging, the largest network in the fediverse. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.joinmastodon.android/)</sub>
    *   **[Mastodon Apps](https://joinmastodon.org/apps)** - Official directory of Mastodon apps for Android, iOS, web and desktop.
*   **[Pleroma](https://git.pleroma.social/pleroma/pleroma)** - Lightweight federated social network server. <sub>ActivityPub</sub>
    *   **[Soapbox](https://gitlab.com/soapbox-pub/soapbox-fe)** - Web frontend for Pleroma focused on custom branding and ease of use.

##### Twitter / Alternative Frontends

*   **[Nitter (⭐14k)](https://github.com/zedeus/nitter)** - Twitter frontend without JavaScript or ads that proxies every request; see the [instances list (⭐14k)](https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/wiki/Instances). `💀 inactive` <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Squawker (⭐1.5k)](https://github.com/j-fbriere/squawker)** - Android Twitter client with no account, tracking or ads, keeping subscriptions on the device; the maintained fork of Fritter. <sub>Android · MIT · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.ca.squawker/)</sub>

##### Reddit / Alternative Frontends

*   **Reddit**: the official site and apps are full of trackers and ads. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/194)</sub>

##### Reddit / Reddit alternatives

*   **[Lemmy](https://join-lemmy.org/)** - Federated link aggregator and discussion forum, organized into communities like subreddits. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Mbin](https://joinmbin.org/)** - Federated link aggregator with microblogging, the community-run continuation of kbin. <sub>ActivityPub · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Streaming Platforms (Twitch) / Privacy respecting Reddit clients

*   **Twitch**: owned by Amazon and funded by ads, so viewers are tracked for advertising. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/200)</sub>
*   **[Owncast](https://owncast.online/)** - Self-hosted live streaming server with built-in chat, so you own the stream and your viewers' data. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>

##### Streaming Platforms (Twitch) / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **[Twire (⭐1.3k)](https://github.com/twireapp/Twire)** - Android Twitch client with built-in ad blocking, VODs with chat replay, and optional login. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.perflyst.twire/)</sub>

##### Imgur / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **Imgur**: 23 trackers in its Android app, and a site heavy with ads. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/325) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.imgur.mobile/latest/)</sub>
*   **[rimgo](https://codeberg.org/rimgo/rimgo)** - Read-only Imgur frontend that works without JavaScript; see the [instances](https://rimgo.codeberg.page/).

##### IMDb / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **IMDb**: owned by Amazon, with 8 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/313) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.imdb.mobile/latest/)</sub>
*   **[libremdb (⭐441)](https://github.com/zyachel/libremdb)** - IMDb frontend without ads or tracking that proxies images and video through the instance. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Fandom / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **Fandom**: wikis loaded with ads and autoplaying video, and 8 trackers in the app. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2256) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.fandom.app/latest/)</sub>

##### Commenting Engines / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **Disqus**: loads trackers on every site that embeds it, and logs commenters' IP, device and location. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/239)</sub>
*   **[Isso](https://isso-comments.de)** - Lightweight self-hosted comment server, meant as a drop-in replacement for Disqus. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>

##### Translation / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **DeepL**: the free version stores your texts and may use them to train its models. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2073)</sub>

##### Text translation / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **[LibreTranslate](https://libretranslate.com/)** - Self-hosted machine translation API and web app with no ties to proprietary services. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Alternative Google Translate frontends / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **[Mozhi](https://codeberg.org/aryak/mozhi)** - Frontend that proxies Google Translate, DeepL, Yandex and other engines behind one interface. <sub>self-host</sub>
*   **[SimplyTranslate](https://simplytranslate.org/)** - Frontend that proxies your requests to Google Translate and other engines. <sub>self-host</sub>

##### RSS Readers / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **[Capy Reader (⭐1.4k)](https://github.com/jocmp/capyreader)** - Small RSS reader, local or synced with Miniflux and FreshRSS. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.capyreader.app/) (Play Store version has trackers)</sub>
*   **[Feeder (⭐3.1k)](https://github.com/spacecowboy/Feeder)** - RSS reader that fetches feeds directly on your device, with no account. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Fluent Reader (⭐9.8k)](https://github.com/yang991178/fluent-reader)** - Desktop RSS reader that keeps your feeds locally, with optional sync to self-hosted services. <sub>Linux · Windows · macOS · BSD-3-Clause</sub>
*   **[FreshRSS](https://freshrss.org/)** - Self-hosted feed aggregator with a web interface, multi-user support and an API for mobile apps. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Miniflux](https://miniflux.app/)** - Minimalist self-hosted feed reader with no tracking, written in Go. <sub>self-host · Apache-2.0</sub>
*   **[NetNewsWire](https://netnewswire.com/)** - RSS reader for Mac, iPhone and iPad that works locally or syncs with self-hosted services. <sub>macOS · iOS · MIT</sub>
*   **[Newsboat](https://newsboat.org/)** - RSS reader for the terminal, configured with plain text files. <sub>Linux · macOS · CLI · MIT</sub>
*   **[NewsFlash](https://gitlab.com/news-flash/news_flash_gtk)** - RSS reader for Linux that works locally or with self-hosted services such as Miniflux and FreshRSS. <sub>Linux · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Read You (⭐7.6k)](https://github.com/ReadYouApp/ReadYou)** - Material You RSS reader, local or synced with self-hosted services. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### VPNs / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **Free VPNs**: someone pays for the servers, usually you, through logs and data sold to advertisers. <sub>[Source](https://thenextweb.com/news/be-cautious-free-vpns-are-selling-your-data-to-3rd-parties)</sub>
*   **[SPN](https://safing.io/spn/)** - Paid add-on to the Portmaster firewall that routes each app's connections over separate multi-hop paths. <sub>Linux · Windows · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Web Browser / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **Opera**: 6 trackers in its Android app, and owned by a Chinese consortium since 2016. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.opera.browser/latest/) · [Source](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opera_\(web_browser\))</sub>

##### Android / iOS / Privacy respecting Twitch clients

*   **[Cromite (⭐8.5k)](https://github.com/uazo/cromite)** - Chromium fork, successor to Bromite, with built-in ad blocking and anti-fingerprinting measures. <sub>Android · Linux · Windows · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Firefox](https://www.firefox.com/en-US/mobile/)** - Mozilla's browser, with its own engine on Android and Enhanced Tracking Protection on by default. `⚠️ website trackers` `⚠️ telemetry opt-out`
    *   **[IronFox](https://gitlab.com/ironfox-oss/IronFox)** - Hardened Firefox for Android with proprietary blobs removed, a fork of the discontinued Mull. <sub>Android</sub>
*   **[Privacy Browser](https://www.stoutner.com/privacy-browser-android/)** - Browser that keeps JavaScript, cookies and other tracking features off until you allow them per site. <sub>Android</sub>
*   **[Vanadium](https://grapheneos.org/features#vanadium)** - Hardened Chromium from the GrapheneOS project, and the default browser on GrapheneOS. <sub>Android</sub>

##### Browser Addons / Anti-tracking

*   **[LibRedirect](https://libredirect.manerakai.com/)** - Redirects YouTube, Reddit, Google Maps and other sites to privacy-friendly front ends, successor to Privacy Redirect. <sub>Browser · GPL-3.0</sub>
    *   **[Predirect (⭐279)](https://github.com/pritkr/predirect)** - Similar extension built on Manifest V3 with minimal permissions, for Chromium browsers that no longer run older extensions. <sub>Browser · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[uBlock Origin (⭐69k)](https://github.com/gorhill/uBlock)** - Light, efficient ad and tracker blocker, in full only on Firefox since Chrome dropped Manifest V2 extensions. <sub>Browser · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Browser Addons / Useful Tools

*   **[SingleFile (⭐23k)](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)** - Saves a complete web page as a single HTML file you can open offline. <sub>Browser · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Domains & Hosting / Useful Tools

*   **[OrangeWebsite](https://orangewebsite.com/)** - Icelandic web host focused on free speech, with anonymous signup and cryptocurrency or cash payment. `⚠️ website trackers`

##### Link Shorteners / Useful Tools

*   **Bitly**: built to track every click for marketing analytics. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/224)</sub>
*   **[Kutt](https://kutt.to/)** - Shortener with custom domains and password-protected links that you can host yourself. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[YOURLS](https://yourls.org/)** - Self-hosted shortener written in PHP, with its own click stats and a plugin system. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>

##### Captchas / Useful Tools

*   **hCaptcha**: collects device and browsing data to score visitors, under a poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2207)</sub>
*   **[ALTCHA](https://altcha.org)** - Self-hosted proof-of-work captcha with no cookies or tracking. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[mCaptcha](https://mcaptcha.org/)** - Proof-of-work captcha that rate-limits bots by making each request cost some computation. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Private Captcha](https://privatecaptcha.com)** - EU-made proof-of-work captcha, as a hosted service or on your own server. `⚠️ source-available` <sub>self-host</sub>

##### 2FA / Useful Tools

*   **Authy**: needs your phone number, and a 2024 leak exposed 33 million of them. <sub>[Source](https://www.malwarebytes.com/blog/news/2024/07/authy-phone-numbers-accessed-by-cybercriminals-warns-twilio)</sub>
*   **Microsoft Authenticator**: 5 trackers in its Android app, and no way to export your tokens to another app. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.azure.authenticator/latest/)</sub>
*   **[2FAS Auth](https://2fas.com/auth)** - Authenticator whose browser extension asks your phone for a code and fills it in, no account needed. <sub>GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Aegis](https://getaegis.app/)** - Encrypted token vault that imports from most other authenticators and exports in plain or encrypted form. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.beemdevelopment.aegis/)</sub>
*   **[ente Auth](https://ente.com/auth/)** - Authenticator with end-to-end encrypted sync between your devices, from the makers of ente Photos. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[FreeOTPPlus (⭐750)](https://github.com/helloworld1/FreeOTPPlus)** - Fork of FreeOTP with backups, search and dark mode. <sub>Android · Apache-2.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.liberty.android.freeotpplus/)</sub>
*   **[Stratum (⭐4.6k)](https://github.com/stratumauth/app)** - Authenticator with Wear OS support and encrypted backups. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Password Managers / Useful Tools

*   **LastPass**: a 2022 breach leaked customers' encrypted vaults and, unencrypted, the sites they saved. <sub>[Source](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_LastPass_data_breach)</sub>
*   **[KeePassXC](https://keepassxc.org/)** - Offline password manager that keeps your vault in an encrypted file you control, with no account or sync service. <sub>Linux · macOS · Windows</sub>
    *   **[KeePassDX](https://www.keepassdx.com/)** - KeePass-compatible password manager for Android. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.kunzisoft.keepass.libre/)</sub>
    *   **[Strongbox](https://strongboxsafe.com/)** - KeePass-compatible password manager for iPhone, iPad and Mac. <sub>iOS · macOS · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Encryption / Useful Tools

*   **BitLocker**: with device encryption, Windows uploads your recovery key to your Microsoft account. <sub>[Source](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/security/operating-system-security/data-protection/bitlocker/)</sub>
*   **[age](https://age-encryption.org)** - Simple, modern file encryption with small keys and nothing to configure. <sub>CLI · BSD-3-Clause</sub>
*   **[Cryptomator](https://cryptomator.org/)** - Encrypts files on your device before they reach any cloud storage provider. <sub>GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.cryptomator.lite/)</sub>
*   **[Shufflecake](https://shufflecake.net/)** - Hidden encrypted volumes for Linux with plausible deniability, so nobody can prove the hidden data exists. <sub>Linux</sub>
*   **[Stegcloak](https://stegcloak.surge.sh/)** - Hides password-protected secrets inside ordinary text using invisible characters. `⚠️ website trackers` `💀 inactive`
*   **[Tomb](https://dyne.org/software/tomb/)** - Creates and manages encrypted folders on Linux, built on standard LUKS. <sub>Linux · CLI · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[VeraCrypt](https://veracrypt.io/en/Home.html)** - Encrypts whole disks, partitions or file containers, with support for hidden volumes. <sub>Linux · macOS · Windows</sub>

##### Pastebin and Secret Sharing / Useful Tools

*   **[Deleto](https://dele.to)** - Shares passwords and other secrets with client-side encryption and automatic self-destruction.

##### Desktop / Useful Tools

*   **[BusKill](https://www.buskill.in/)** - USB dead man's switch that locks or wipes your computer when the cable is pulled away. <sub>GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[dnscrypt-proxy (⭐14k)](https://github.com/DNSCrypt/dnscrypt-proxy)** - Local DNS proxy with encrypted DNS and anonymized relays, so resolvers can't link your lookups to your IP. <sub>ISC</sub>
*   **[MAT2 (⭐388)](https://github.com/jvoisin/mat2)** - Removes metadata from images, documents, audio and many other file types. <sub>Linux · CLI · LGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Metadata Cleaner](https://gitlab.com/rmnvgr/metadata-cleaner)** - Simple desktop app to view and remove file metadata, built on MAT2. <sub>Linux</sub>
*   **[Mobile Verification Toolkit (⭐16k)](https://github.com/mvt-project/mvt)** - Amnesty International's tool for checking Android and iOS devices for traces of spyware such as Pegasus. `⚠️ source-available` <sub>CLI</sub>
*   **[OpenSnitch (⭐14k)](https://github.com/evilsocket/opensnitch)** - Application firewall that asks before any program connects to the internet. <sub>Linux · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Android / Useful Tools

*   **[NetGuard](https://netguard.me/)** - Blocks internet access per app, no root needed. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/eu.faircode.netguard/)</sub>
*   **[Orbot](https://orbot.app/)** - Routes app traffic through Tor, for all apps or only the ones you choose. Made by the Guardian Project. <sub>Android</sub>
*   **[Plexus](https://plexus.techlore.tech/)** - Community database of which apps work on de-Googled Android. <sub>Android · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/tech.techlore.plexus/)</sub>
*   **[RethinkDNS + Firewall (⭐5.8k)](https://github.com/celzero/rethink-app)** - Firewall and encrypted DNS client in one, no root needed. <sub>Android · Apache-2.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.celzero.bravedns/) (Play Store version has trackers)</sub>
*   **[εxodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/)** - Shows which trackers and permissions an Android app contains, before you install it. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>
    *   **[ClassyShark3xodus](https://f-droid.org/en/packages/com.oF2pks.classyshark3xodus/)** - Checks the apps on your phone for known trackers, using the εxodus database. <sub>Android · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.oF2pks.classyshark3xodus/)</sub>

##### Cloud Storage / Useful Tools

*   **Dropbox**: no end-to-end encryption, and 4 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/270) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.dropbox.android/latest/)</sub>
*   **[Filen](https://filen.io/)** - End-to-end encrypted cloud storage from Germany, with desktop, mobile and web clients you can inspect.
*   **[Sync-in](https://sync-in.com/)** - Self-hosted file storage, sharing and sync with WebDAV, desktop clients and online document editing. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Other useful tools / Useful Tools

*   **[git-annex](https://git-annex.branchable.com/)** - Manages and syncs large files with git without storing their contents in git, across drives and many cloud and offline backends.

##### File Management and Sharing / Useful Tools

*   **WeTransfer**: files aren't end-to-end encrypted, and the site carries trackers. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/214)</sub>
*   **[Blaze](https://blaze.vercel.app/)** - Peer-to-peer file sharing in the browser, between two or more devices at once. `⚠️ website trackers` `💀 inactive` <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[Croc (⭐41k)](https://github.com/schollz/croc)** - Sends files between two computers with end-to-end encryption, using a short code phrase. <sub>CLI · MIT</sub>
*   **[Gokapi (⭐3k)](https://github.com/Forceu/Gokapi)** - Lightweight self-hosted alternative to Firefox Send, where only you can upload and links expire. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Localsend](https://localsend.org/)** - Shares files with nearby devices over your local network, with no internet connection or account needed. <sub>Apache-2.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.localsend.localsend_app/)</sub>
*   **[Lufi](https://framagit.org/fiat-tux/hat-softwares/lufi)** - File sharing server that encrypts uploads in your browser, so the server never sees the contents. <sub>self-host</sub>
*   **[Magic Wormhole (⭐23k)](https://github.com/magic-wormhole/magic-wormhole)** - Sends files from one computer to another with end-to-end encryption, using a short one-time code. <sub>CLI · MIT</sub>
*   **[OnionShare (⭐7.2k)](https://github.com/onionshare/onionshare)** - Shares files, hosts websites and runs chat rooms over Tor onion services, anonymously and without a third-party server.
*   **[PairDrop (⭐12k)](https://github.com/schlagmichdoch/PairDrop)** - Snapdrop fork that shares files in the browser on your local network, and with paired devices beyond it. <sub>fork · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Paperless-ngx (⭐47k)](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx)** - Self-hosted document manager that scans, indexes and archives your paperwork on your own server. <sub>self-host · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[QRcp (⭐11k)](https://github.com/claudiodangelis/qrcp)** - Transfers files over Wi-Fi from your computer to your phone by scanning a QR code in the terminal. <sub>CLI · MIT</sub>
*   **[scrt.link](https://scrt.link/file)** - End-to-end encrypted file transfer of up to 100 GB, stored in Switzerland for up to 30 days. <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[Send](https://gitlab.com/timvisee/send)** - Fork of Firefox Send that encrypts files in the browser and expires links after a set time or number of downloads. <sub>self-host · fork</sub>
*   **[Snapdrop](https://snapdrop.me)** - Local file sharing in the browser, inspired by Apple's AirDrop. <sub>self-host · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Winden](https://winden.app/)** - Magic Wormhole in the browser, for end-to-end encrypted transfers without installing anything. <sub>MIT</sub>

##### Download Manager / Useful Tools

*   **[Motrix](https://motrix.app)** - Full-featured download manager for HTTP, FTP, BitTorrent and magnet links. <sub>Desktop</sub>
*   **[Persepolis Download Manager](https://persepolisdm.github.io)** - Graphical front end for aria2 with download scheduling and video grabbing. <sub>Desktop · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Xtreme Download Manager](https://xtremedownloadmanager.com/)** - Download accelerator that resumes broken downloads and saves streaming video from many sites. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>Desktop · GPL-3.0</sub>

##### Bookmarking / Useful Tools

*   **Evernote Web Clipper**: saves pages to Evernote without end-to-end encryption, under a poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/207)</sub>
*   **[Karakeep](https://karakeep.com/)** - Self-hosted app for saving links, notes and images, with full-text search and optional AI tagging, formerly Hoarder. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Notes and Tasks / Useful Tools

*   **Notion**: no end-to-end encryption, so Notion can read everything in your workspace. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/1817)</sub>
*   **[Lockbook](https://lockbook.net)** - End-to-end encrypted notebook for notes, documents and drawings, as an Evernote alternative. <sub>Unlicense</sub>
*   **[OpenHabitTracker](https://openhabittracker.net/)** - Habit, task and note tracker that keeps your data on the device, or syncs to a server you run yourself. `⚠️ website trackers`
*   **[Super Productivity](https://super-productivity.com)** - To-do list and time tracker that keeps data local with no account, with optional sync over WebDAV or Dropbox. <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[Tasks.org](https://tasks.org)** - To-do and task manager that works offline, with CalDAV sync. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.tasks/) (Play Store version has trackers)</sub>

##### Office / Useful Tools

*   **[Collabora Office](https://www.collaboraonline.com/collabora-office/)** - LibreOffice-based office suite for desktop and mobile, from the team behind Collabora Online.
*   **[CryptPad](https://cryptpad.org/)** - Collaborative documents, spreadsheets and more, end-to-end encrypted so the server can't read them. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### PDF Tools / Useful Tools

*   **iLovePDF**: uploads your documents to its servers, and the Android app has 7 trackers. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/7842) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.ilovepdf.www/latest/)</sub>
*   **Smallpdf**: uploads your documents to its servers, and the Android app has 3 trackers. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/6244) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.smallpdf.app.android/latest/)</sub>
*   **[BentoPDF](https://bentopdf.com/)** - Browser-based PDF toolkit to merge, split, compress, sign and redact, with files processed on your device. <sub>Web · self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Calendar / Useful Tools

*   **[Etar (⭐2.6k)](https://github.com/Etar-Group/Etar-Calendar)** - Calendar app that works with any CalDAV server through a sync adapter. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/ws.xsoh.etar/)</sub>
*   **[Fossify Calendar (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/FossifyOrg/Calendar)** - Offline calendar with widgets and no ads, which can also sync with your device's CalDAV accounts. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.fossify.calendar/)</sub>
*   **[KashCal (⭐469)](https://github.com/KashCal/KashCal)** - Offline-first calendar with built-in iCloud and CalDAV sync, full-text search and widgets. <sub>Android · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/org.onekash.kashcal/)</sub>
*   **[Nextcloud Calendar](https://apps.nextcloud.com/apps/calendar)** - Calendar app for your Nextcloud server, with CalDAV sync to your other devices. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Proton Calendar](https://proton.me/calendar)** - End-to-end encrypted calendar from the makers of Proton Mail.

##### Media Streaming Platforms / Useful Tools

*   **Netflix**: logs what and how you watch, under a poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/185)</sub>
*   **Amazon Prime Video**: 3 trackers in its Android app, under a poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2444) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.amazon.avod.thirdpartyclient/latest/)</sub>
*   **Disney Plus**: a very poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/2745)</sub>
*   **SoundCloud**: 13 trackers in its Android app, under a very poorly rated privacy policy. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/276) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.soundcloud.android/latest/)</sub>

##### Podcasts / Deezer alternative clients

*   **Spotify**: a very poorly rated privacy policy that covers your mood and friends, and 4 trackers. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/225) · [Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.spotify.music/latest/)</sub>
*   **[Funkwhale](https://www.funkwhale.audio/)** - Federated platform for hosting, sharing and listening to podcasts and music. <sub>self-host · ActivityPub</sub>
*   **iVoox**: 13 trackers in its Android app, and more on its website. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.ivoox.app/latest/)</sub>
*   **Deezer**: 11 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/deezer.android.app/latest/)</sub>
*   **[AntennaPod](https://antennapod.org)** - Podcast player that subscribes to any RSS feed, with no account needed. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/de.danoeh.antennapod/)</sub>

##### Audio / Useful Tools

*   **[Ampache](https://ampache.org/)** - Web-based server for streaming your own music and video collection. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Koel](https://koel.dev/)** - Personal music streaming server with a web player. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[mucke (⭐268)](https://github.com/moritz-weber/mucke)** - Player for local music files with custom playback rules. <sub>Android · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/rocks.mucke/)</sub>
*   **[Navidrome](https://www.navidrome.org/)** - Lightweight music server for your own collection, compatible with Subsonic apps. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>self-host · GPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[Nuclear](https://nuclearplayer.com/)** - Desktop music player that streams from free sources, with no account. <sub>Linux · macOS · Windows · AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Audio / Spotify alternative clients

*   **[ncspot (⭐6.8k)](https://github.com/hrkfdn/ncspot)** - Terminal Spotify client written in Rust, inspired by ncmpc. Needs Spotify Premium. <sub>CLI · BSD-2-Clause</sub>
*   **[psst (⭐9.5k)](https://github.com/jpochyla/psst)** - Fast Spotify client with a native GUI written in Rust. Needs Spotify Premium. <sub>Linux · macOS · Windows · MIT</sub>
*   **[Spotube](https://spotube.cc/)** - Plugin-based music player that can load your Spotify playlists and stream the audio from other sources, no Premium needed. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Audio / YouTube Music alternative clients

*   **[SimpMusic](https://simpmusic.org/)** - YouTube Music client with no ads and SponsorBlock built in, successor to the discontinued ViMusic and RiMusic. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>Android · Desktop · GPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.maxrave.simpmusic/)</sub>

##### Home Assistants / Other OS

*   **Alexa**: an always-listening microphone that sends your requests to Amazon. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/190)</sub>
*   **Siri**: Apple contractors regularly listened to Siri recordings, including private moments. <sub>[Source](https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2019/jul/26/apple-contractors-regularly-hear-confidential-details-on-siri-recordings)</sub>
*   **[OpenVoiceOS](https://www.openvoiceos.org/)** - Voice assistant you run on your own hardware, a community successor to Mycroft. <sub>Apache-2.0</sub>

*   **Shazam**: owned by Apple, and its Android app carries Google's analytics trackers. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.shazam.android/latest/)</sub>
*   **SoundHound**: 11 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.melodis.midomiMusicIdentifier.freemium/latest/)</sub>
*   **Musixmatch**: 8 trackers in its Android app. <sub>[Exodus](https://reports.exodus-privacy.eu.org/en/reports/com.musixmatch.android.lyrify/latest/)</sub>

##### Android Gallery / Plugins for Minecraft

*   **Amazon Photos**: Amazon gathers data from your library, as its own list of examples shows. <sub>[Source](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html?nodeId=GX7NJQ4ZB8MHFRNJ)</sub>
*   **Google Photos**: scans your photos, and once got a father reported to police over pictures taken for a doctor. <sub>[Source](https://petapixel.com/2022/08/22/google-flags-photos-of-fathers-sick-son-as-child-abuse-informs-police/)</sub>

##### Self-hosted / Deezer alternative clients

*   **[Immich](https://immich.app/)** - Self-hosted photo and video backup straight from your phone, the closest match to Google Photos. <sub>self-host · AGPL-3.0 · [F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/app.alextran.immich/)</sub>
*   **[PhotoPrism](https://www.photoprism.app/)** - Self-hosted photo library with automatic tagging and face recognition that runs on your own server. <sub>self-host</sub>

##### Minecraft / Deezer alternative clients

##### Developer Tools / Other OS

*   **[CrowdSec](https://www.crowdsec.net/)** - Collaborative intrusion prevention for servers, like fail2ban with crowd-sourced blocklists. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[Hetty](https://hetty.xyz/)** - HTTP toolkit for security research, as an alternative to Burp Suite Pro. <sub>MIT</sub>

##### IDEs / Other OS

##### Version Control / Other OS

*   **GitHub**: owned by Microsoft, and public code hosted there was used to train Copilot. <sub>[ToS;DR](https://tosdr.org/en/service/297)</sub>
*   **[Gitea](https://about.gitea.com/)** - Lightweight self-hosted Git forge, and the project Forgejo was forked from. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>

##### Remote Access and Control / Other OS

##### ChatGPT / Other OS

*   **[Jan](https://jan.ai/)** - Desktop chat app that runs open models offline, with optional connections to cloud providers.
*   **[LibreChat](https://www.librechat.ai/)** - Self-hosted chat interface that puts many AI providers and local models behind one UI you control. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[LocalAI](https://localai.io/)** - Self-hosted OpenAI-compatible API that runs language, image and voice models on your own hardware. <sub>self-host · MIT</sub>
*   **[ollama](https://ollama.com/)** - Runs open models locally with one command; its optional cloud models can be switched off entirely. <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[PasteGuard](https://pasteguard.com/)** - Proxy that masks personal data and secrets before your prompts reach a cloud AI provider, then restores them in the reply. <sub>self-host · Apache-2.0</sub>

*   **[FunASR (⭐21k)](https://github.com/modelscope/FunASR)** - Local speech recognition toolkit with voice detection, punctuation and speaker labels; it downloads models from ModelScope by default. <sub>MIT</sub>

*   **[Nextcloud PhoneTrack](https://apps.nextcloud.com/apps/phonetrack)** - Nextcloud app for your location history, fed by its [Android app](https://gitlab.com/eneiluj/phonetrack-android) or [other loggers (⭐183)](https://github.com/julien-nc/phonetrack/blob/main/doc/user.md#logging-methods), which can cache positions offline and upload them in batches. <sub>AGPL-3.0</sub>

*   **[Deskreen](https://deskreen.com/)** - Turns any device with a web browser into a second screen for your computer over the local network. `⚠️ website trackers` <sub>Desktop · AGPL-3.0</sub>
*   **[JustDeleteMe](https://justdeleteme.xyz/)** - Directory of direct links for deleting your accounts from web services, rated by difficulty; sister project [JustGetMyData (⭐169)](https://github.com/daviddavo/jgmd) covers getting your data out. <sub>MIT</sub>
*   **[OpenTabletDriver](https://opentabletdriver.net/)** - User-mode driver for drawing tablets that replaces vendor drivers, some of which [report every app you open](https://robertheaton.com/2020/02/05/wacom-drawing-tablets-track-name-of-every-application-you-open/). <sub>Desktop · LGPL-3.0</sub>

##### Privacy vs Security vs Anonymity / Apps and services

*   Privacy is control over your personal information: knowing what is collected about you, and deciding who can access it and how.
*   Security is protection of your information from unauthorized access or theft, so attackers can't get at it.
*   Anonymity means your actions can't be traced back to you. Someone may see what is done, but not who did it.

##### Legend / Apps and services

*   `💀 inactive`: development has stalled. It still works and has no maintained alternative, so check before relying on it.
*   `⚠️ closed source`: the code isn't public. Listed only because of a long, trusted record.
*   `⚠️ source-available`: the code is public, but the license isn't an open source one.
*   `⚠️ website trackers`: the project's website loads third-party trackers. That says nothing about the app itself.
*   `⚠️ telemetry opt-out`: the app sends usage data by default, but a setting turns it off.
*   `⚠️ closed server` and `⚠️ account required`: the server code isn't public, or an account is needed to use it.

#### [15. Awesome Lowcode](/content/antdimot/awesome-lowcode/README.md)

##### AI

*   [kdpbook.io](https://kdpbook.io/?utm_source=awesome-lowcode\&utm_medium=github) - No-code AI book studio: describe a book in a chat and get it written, illustrated and typeset, with the Amazon KDP print PDF, full-wrap cover, Kindle eBook and listing sheet.
*   [Pinegen AI](https://www.pinegen.ai/) - Generate, fix, and backtest compiler-validated Pine Script v6 strategies for TradingView using AI. Free to start, no coding needed.

##### Automation

*   [Upload-Post](https://www.upload-post.com) - Social media publishing API for posting and scheduling to TikTok, Instagram, YouTube, LinkedIn, X, and more. Integrates with Zapier, Make, and n8n, and has an MCP server. Free plan available.

##### Educational

*   [Benian n8n Automation Catalog](https://benian-technologies.github.io/automation-templates/) - n8n workflow examples with setup guides and runnable walkthroughs using fictional data.

##### Flow-based programming

*   [Ciaren](https://ciaren.com) - Open-source, local-first visual ETL and ML workflow builder that exports readable pandas or Polars code.

##### Marketing

*   [thirds.ai](https://thirds.ai/) - Make branded images and PDFs from editable templates in a studio, AI assistant, or API.
*   [Stripo](https://stripo.email) - No-code email design platform with 1,690+ templates and export to 90+ ESPs.

##### Online database creator apps

*   [Nhost](https://nhost.io) - Open source Postgres backend with a dashboard for tables and permissions, an instant GraphQL API, auth, storage and functions.
*   [PHPRunner](https://xlinesoft.com/phprunner/index.htm) - Commercial low-code builder for web applications using existing databases, with forms, reports, permissions, and deployment to your own web server.

##### Visual programming

*   [Figment.so](https://figment.so/) - Publish Figma designs as websites through a Figma plugin.
*   [Helmpages](https://helmpages.com/) - Websites for independent professionals and small businesses, built and kept current by sending a message. Paid, with a free trial.
*   [HtmlDrag](https://htmldrag.com/) - Visual editor for existing and AI-generated HTML pages, with drag-and-drop editing, element-level AI refinement, and HTML export.
*   [BuzzBlender Kiosk Editor](https://kiosk.buzzblender.com/) - No-code kiosk interface builder for creating button-based experiences with video, images and text, with preview and standalone HTML export.

##### Misc

*   [BESSER (⭐200)](https://github.com/BESSER-PEARL/BESSER) - Open-source Python low-code platform: draw a UML model in a web editor and generate FastAPI, React, Django and SQLAlchemy code.
*   [BESSER Agentic Framework (⭐71)](https://github.com/BESSER-PEARL/BESSER-Agentic-Framework) - Design and implement agents and chatbots in Python.

#### [23. Awesome Testing](/content/TheJambo/awesome-testing/README.md)

##### Software / API Testing

*   [Bifurc (⭐4)](https://github.com/HarshalKudale/bifurc) - Local-first API client, mock server and traffic proxy for macOS, Windows and Linux, with a CLI for CI.

##### Software / AI & LLM Testing

*   [llmdrill (⭐0)](https://github.com/hamarshehmhmd/llmdrill) - Open-source CLI that runs your app behind a local fake of the Anthropic and OpenAI APIs, injects outages (529/503 overloads, 429 with retry-after, hangs, cut streams) and grades its retries, backoff, timeouts and failover.

##### Software / UI & End-to-End Testing

*   [GenClass (⭐7)](https://github.com/genclass-dev/GenClass) - Open-source browser library that reports race conditions in a web app while they happen (stale responses written over newer data, duplicate submits, failure storms) with a small on-device model. Its local MCP server re-runs a page with responses delivered in different orders to find races and replays one order to prove a fix.
*   [mendwright (⭐0)](https://github.com/saadbuilt/mendwright) - Self-healing selectors for Playwright and Selenium in Python. Repairs a broken selector from a saved fingerprint of the element, asks a model only when that isn't enough, checks the repair on the live page and can write it back into your code.
*   [Replay QA](https://replay.io/) - Autonomous QA agent for web apps built on Replay's time-travel runtime recording and analysis. It explores an app from its URL, writes and runs Playwright tests, and delivers a root-cause analysis for each failure, backed by evidence from the recorded execution. Free tier, no credit card.

##### Software / Test Data Management

*   [Brume (⭐7)](https://github.com/brumeorg/Brume) - Copies a subset of a PostgreSQL database to a test environment, pseudonymizing personal data and keeping foreign keys valid.

##### Software / Test Automation Frameworks

*   [bareenv (⭐0)](https://github.com/Blasword/bareenv) - Runs a command once per environment combination (unset, empty, invalid) and reports the ones that break.

#### [24. Collective Ai Tools](/content/Hyraze/collective-ai-tools/README.md)

##### Developer Tools

*   [Browser Agent Blueprint (⭐1)](https://github.com/LydiaTools/browser-agent-blueprint) - Open-source browser agent prompt modules and synthetic recovery examples for uncertain actions. `#free` `#opensource`
*   [Keenable](https://keenable.ai) - Independent web search index for AI agents: search and page fetch over API, CLI and MCP (`https://api.keenable.ai/mcp`), with 100K free requests a month and keyless public endpoints. `#freemium` `#api` `#mcp`
*   [Model Salt](https://modelsalt.com/) - Open source MCP server with one tool: before a creative task the model gets a few choices drawn from atmospheric and quantum noise, so one prompt gives different answers that still do what was asked. `#free` `#opensource` `#mcp`
*   [SoloPup Memory (⭐1)](https://github.com/OakcoderX/solopup-memory) - Native DeepSeek Harness text-memory beta with source references, project scope and correction history; requires Node >=24.9 and DSH >=0.2.1-rc.0 <0.3.0, and configured model APIs may charge. `#opensource` `#memory`

##### Image Editing

*   [Clarpex AI](https://clarpex.ai/) - Free online AI photo fixer to unblur, restore old photos, colorize black and white photos, remove glare and upscale to 4K or 8K, with daily free credits and no sign-up. `#freemium` `#photo`

##### Image Generator

*   [MakeImg](https://makeimg.ai/) - AI photo generator that turns a text prompt or reference photo into a new image, with 11 models and free daily credits. `#freemium` `#image`
*   [QuestStudio](https://queststudio.io/) - Generate and edit images, then continue related video, voice, and music workflows in a connected creative studio. `#paid` `#design`

##### Video

*   [RefVid](https://refvid.com/) - Generates AI videos from 1–5 reference photos of a character or product, using the photos to help maintain character or product consistency. New accounts get one free 5-second clip, then paid credits or plans. `#paid`

##### Writing

*   [CoverNice](https://covernice.com/) - KDP cover studio: five directions, spine width from page count, 300 dpi wrap PDF. `#freemium`

*   [claude-guard (⭐0)](https://github.com/jokerlin/claude-guard) - checks your egress IP before [Claude Code](https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code) starts. If the IP that claude.ai sees is not on your allowlist, claude stops and asks before it connects.
*   [sprout (⭐1)](https://github.com/beenjii14/sprout) - Turn a Jira ticket into a clean `git` branch.
    One command. A short, descriptive name. Zero copy-pasting.
*   [walh-shell (⭐2)](https://github.com/casonadams/walh-shell) - A fast, modern, and extensible Zsh (and Bash) plugin for instant terminal color theme switching that lets you instantly change your terminal’s color scheme with a single command.

#### [30. Magictools](/content/ellisonleao/magictools/README.md)

##### Graphics / Assets/Placeholders

*   :money\_with\_wings: [Gridwright Studio](https://gridwright.store/) - Subscription library of pixel-art game asset packs (tilesets, characters, UI, icons, effects, isometric, music and SFX) for any engine, many with Godot scenes and Tiled tilesets. Free samples with a free account. Drawn in code with AI-assisted tools.
*   :free: [Minecraft Pixel Art](https://minecraft-pixel-art.com/editor/blueprint) - Browser image-to-Minecraft block blueprints with material lists, PNG plans and Java structure NBT export; no account required.

##### Graphics / Spritesheet Tools

*   :free: [SpriteCanvas Sprite Sheet Tools](https://spritecanvas.com/tools/) - Free browser tools that slice a sprite sheet into frames, pack images into a sheet, convert GIFs to sheets and back, and turn MP4/WebM clips into sheets with a JSON atlas. Files stay on your device; no account needed.

##### Graphics / Animation

*   :money\_with\_wings: [SlotVFX](https://slotvfx.com/) - Desktop editor for particles and game VFX with Spine support. Free to use; exports for Pixi, Phaser, Cocos Creator and WebGL2 need a paid plan.

##### Graphics / Modeling

*   :money\_with\_wings: [Unity Asset Exporter](https://extractunitypackage.com) - Open .unitypackage files in the browser without Unity and export models as textured GLB.

##### AI / Voxel Editors

*   :money\_with\_wings: [Flockbay](https://flockbay.com/?utm_source=magictools\&utm_medium=directory\&utm_campaign=listing) - Free AI game maker built on Godot: a desktop app where an AI agent builds and playtests a Godot 4 project on your disk, on free hosted AI, your own Claude or ChatGPT account, or your own agent over MCP. Optional paid plans add hosted AI usage.
*   :money\_with\_wings: [SpriteCanvas](https://spritecanvas.com/) - AI sprite animation generator for 2D games: make a character from text or an image, animate idle, walk, run, jump and attack moves, and export transparent PNG sprite sheets with a JSON atlas for Phaser, PixiJS and Godot. Free daily credits.

#### [31. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### APIs, Data, and ML

*   [TimeLogic API](https://timelogicapi.com) - World time and timezone API with cryptographically signed JSON responses, so you can verify the time wasn't tampered with. Get the current time by timezone, IP address or coordinates, convert between zones, check DST changes, and do business-day date math. WorldTimeAPI-compatible endpoints for apps that used worldtimeapi.org. Free plan includes 10,000 requests/month on all endpoints.

##### Security and PKI

*   [CyberShield Studio](https://cybershieldstudio.com/) - Security monitoring for e-commerce store operators and developers. Weekly scans for JavaScript changes or injection, tracking before consent, security header settings, and vulnerable client-side libraries and WordPress plugins. Free plan monitors 1 store (home page + checkout path) with 30 days of history. No credit card required.

##### Monitoring

*   [Gairė](https://gaire.lt/en) - Web Change monitoring for research, price changes, out-of/in stock monitoring. Free plan up to 10 pages checked every hour, alerts by sms, email, Webhook, Slack/Discord/Telegram integrations. No card needed.
*   [hypermonitors.com](https://hypermonitors.com) - Monitors your infrastructure from three different locations (ZH - NYC - SG) with every outage checked from a 2nd location before alerts go out. Free forever for 5 monitors and 5 minute checks.
*   [PingWise](https://pingwise.com/) - Quorum-based uptime monitoring, using 15 probes in 6 different continents. Pricing is credit based, offering 650,000 free checks per month (translates to 75 free monitors at 5-minute checks). No credit card, unlimited users.
*   [RealUptime](https://realuptime.io) - Uptime checks from 4 regions every 3 minutes, a public status page with 100 subscribers, and API, CLI, Terraform and MCP access. Free for 10 monitors, commercial use allowed, no card required.

##### Crash and Exception Handling

*   [RealUptime Errors](https://realuptime.io/errors) - Error tracking that works with Sentry SDKs. Free for 10,000 events a month with 14-day retention; past the cap, events are refused and counted, never billed.

##### Forms

*   [whatayarn](https://whatayarn.com/) - Online form builder. Unlimited forms and responses on Free, subject to fair use.

##### Generative AI

*   [evalship](https://evalship.com) - find the flaws in your LLM evals. Reviews your evals suite for improvement opportunities, monitors your PRs for any eval regressions or unintended side effects and optimizes your prompts and models based on experiments in your product. Free for 1 private repo for 1 user, 100 PR analyses a month, 30 days of eval history. Free for public repos.

##### PaaS

*   [EthioDeploy](https://ethiodeploy.com/) - Hosting and agentic coding platform containing Built-in Analytics, Database, GitHub CI/CD, free sub-domain, CLI, AI coding agent. Free tier allows one project 256 MB RAM / 0.5 CPU, sleeps after 30 min idle, 512 MB database and 100,000 tokens on the default model one time.

##### Issue Tracking and Project Management

*   [Archboard](https://getarchboard.app/) - Architecture diagrams your team and AI agents edit live. Free plan: 1 seat, 3 diagrams, all built-in node packs and live editing with your AI agents (MCP).
*   [UserJot](https://userjot.com) - Live chat, feedback boards, a roadmap, and a changelog, so everything your customers say reaches the people and agents building your product. Free plan: Unlimited Posts, Unlimited Users, 3 Admin Roles, 2 Feedback Boards, Roadmap, Changelog.

##### Analytics, Events and Statistics

*   [Datasense](https://usedatasense.com/) - Web analytics with JavaScript error tracking, goals, and revenue attribution. Free plan: 1 site, 5,000 events per month.
*   [Pug](https://pug.sh) - Open-source product analytics with autocapture, trends, funnels, retention, user flows and unified user profiles. Free plan includes 100,000 events per month and 1 year of data retention.

##### Payment and Billing Integration

*   [UsageRail](https://usagerail.com/) - API keys, rate limits, quotas and usage-based billing for paid APIs, checked inside your own server with no gateway. Free up to $1,000/month billed and 1M requests/month.

##### Screenshot APIs

*   [Shotlane](https://shotlane.dev) - Screenshots, clean Markdown and PDFs of any web page, rendered in current Chrome. Hide cookie banners, ads and other popups and many more options. A free account gives you 100 credits per month, no card required.

*   [RIA Africa Mobile Pricing (RAMP) Indices Portal](https://researchictafrica.net/research-ict-africa-ramp-index-2/) - Pricing information on Voice/SMS basket, 1GB data basket, and Value For Money bundles (Q3 2022).
*   [Opera - State of the Mobile Web Africa](https://blogs.opera.com/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/06/SMW_2019.pdf) - Opera reports on mobile web use in Africa (last published 2019). ([2019](https://blogs.opera.com/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/06/SMW_2019.pdf)) ([2016](https://blogs.opera.com/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2016/11/SMWAfrica-Opera-report-2016-01-WEB-1.pdf))
*   [Ericsson Mobility Report - Sub-Saharan Africa](https://www.ericsson.com/en/reports-and-papers/mobility-report/closer-look/sub-saharan-africa) - Regional closer look from the Ericsson Mobility Report. ([2024](https://www.ericsson.com/en/reports-and-papers/mobility-report/closer-look/sub-saharan-africa)) ([2015](https://manypossibilities.net/report-archives/EricssonMobilityReport-nov-2015-regional-report-sub-saharan-africa.pdf))
*   [Opensignal - The State of Mobile Network Experience in Africa](https://www.opensignal.com/2024/11/11/the-state-of-mobile-network-experience-in-africa) - Download speeds and network quality in 27 African countries (November 2024).
*   [Lions go digital: The Internet's transformative potential in Africa](https://www.mckinsey.com/industries/high-tech/our-insights/lions-go-digital-the-internets-transformative-potential-in-africa) - Report by McKinsey on the potential impact of the Internet in Africa (last published 2013).

## Contribution

This repo is generated by [trackawesomelist-source](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source), if you want to add your awesome list here, please edit [config.yml](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/blob/main/config.yml), and send a pull request, or just open an [issue](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/issues), I'll add it manually.

If you want to add badge ([![Track Awesome List](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/badge.svg)](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/ripienaar/free-for-dev/)
) to your awesome list, please add the following code to your README.md:

```markdown
[![Track Awesome List](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/badge.svg)](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/your_repo_pathname/)
```


The doc is still under construction, if you have any question, please open an [issue](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/issues)
